- 60380 nodes · 169551 edges · 1684 communities (1146 shown, 538 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6262 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `705c8ec6`

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
